Okay, then. I'll start:

They started attacking blades of grass and ate giraffes after polymorphing them into bite-size pieces. We went home and ate dinner with inexplicable despair. We couldn't help getting rid of the cook boxes that surrounded us and outnumbered us 5,000 to one. We ran as if we were dead flies that are being bounced by gargantuan infants. Really; don't ask. Michael Jackson came and crushed all the boxes except the box of DOOOOOOM!! But before --- When I woke, I saw mommy being devoured by a fluffy bunny who has driven Escalade's far too often with spicy breath and 24" spinners that blind you. My daddy says that you are really good looking, then he obliterated the wooks with his mom's dog, Trogdor the Burninator, but he got decimated by the Electric Dog Fence. Poor Trogdor now daddy wants a bite-sized pizza with extra cheese. "Eat my shorts," said the grinning Italian Jewish Mafia to my father.
A bullet hit the awful, evil
man named Dave.

We were walking down the street to the eggs that were hatching into cilantro tiny winged donkey-rabbits. They started attacking blades of grass and ate giraffes after polymorphing them into bite-size pieces. We went home and ate dinner with inexplicable despair. We couldn't help getting rid of the cook boxes that surrounded us and outnumbered us 5,000 to one. We ran as if we were dead flies that are being bounced by gargantuan infants. Really; don't ask. Michael Jackson came and crushed all the boxes except the box of DOOOOOOM!! But before --- When I woke, I saw mommy being devoured by a fluffy bunny who has driven Escalade's far too often with spicy breath and 24" spinners that blind you. My daddy says that you are really good looking, then he obliterated the wooks with his mom's dog, Trogdor the Burninator, but he got decimated by the Electric Dog Fence. Poor Trogdor now daddy wants a bite-sized pizza with extra cheese. "Eat my shorts," said the grinning Italian Jewish Mafia to my father. A bullet hit the awful, evil man named Dave "The Pepperoni" Johnson. "You're dead, Dave!" said the Italian Jewish Mafia, but dad took out his giant net and went fishing for big death piranhas. Oh, look, frogs! They're turning yellow! While the king did something stupid, dad had caught the net which had blown up the yellow mother. Meanwhile, in Micronesia, Bill Gates engineered a Republican Convention in 1234567890 seconds. Then he destroyed the democrat party by showing them the tonsils of his dad's parents. However, one democrat's ma said Cheney had hatched the Basilisk found in the butler's coat that was red.Then came a big yellow armadillo which talked in Yiddish resembling German. Reminiscent of many smilies, the purple ninja smiled eight times like this: *biggrin*. Then Mr. Phlart had four orgasms which happened to turn Bowser on somehow. But they came too quickly and gave Bowser his evil clone and some ass. He liked it, but as he shampooed his pants he got a trio of orgasms living in his big fat shell. By this time BOWSER HAD REALISED he was dreaming, because of which he started wetting Toad's giant mushroom-head day after day.
